Questions 19 to 21 are based on the passage you have just heard.

1   What is the secret to living past 100? Playing the piano is among the things the world's oldest person
believes has contributed to her long life. Branyas Morera is now 115 years and 320 days old. That
means she's been alive longer than any other living individual.

活过100岁的秘诀是什么?弹钢琴是世界上最长寿的人认为有助于她长寿的事情之一。Branyas Morera现
在已经115岁零320天了。这意味着她比任何其他在世的人活得都久。

2   Branyas was born in the United States on April 4th, 1907. But her family moved to Spain, where they
were originally from, when she was a young child. She has lived there ever since.
Branyas于1907年4月4日出生在美国。但她的家人在她还是小孩时搬回了他们原来所在的西班牙。从那以
后她就一直住在那里。

3   The 115-year-old is active on social media, regularly sharing aspects of her life. She also shares some
of her secrets to living so long. These include playing the piano from a young age. Unfortunately, she
had to give up the practice seven years ago at 108.
这位115岁的老人活跃在社交媒体上,经常分享她生活的方方面面。她还分享了一些长寿的秘诀。其中包括
从小弹钢琴。不幸的是,她在七年前108岁时不得不放弃了这个练习。

4   Her other secrets for living a long life? She led an active lifestyle until her later years, but has never
followed a specific diet. "Many people ask me what diet I follow to live so many years," Branyas wrote.
"I have always eaten little, but everything, and I have never followed any plan." But she does eat yogurt
every day, which she said has "an infinite number of positive properties" for the body. She has also said
that the secret to a long life is "knowing how to choose" what to forget.
她长寿的其他秘诀?她在晚年之前保持着积极的生活方式,但从未遵循过特定的饮食。"很多人问我遵循什么
饮食才能活这么多年,"Branyas写道。"我一直吃得很少,但什么都吃,从未遵循过任何计划。"但她确实每天
吃酸奶,她说酸奶对身体有"无数积极的作用"。她还说过长寿的秘诀是"知道如何选择"要忘记什么。




                        懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 42 -->

5   "Life would be impossible if we remembered everything," she said.
"如果我们记住一切,生活将是不可能的,"她说。

19. A) She is one of the world's best-known pianists.
     B) She celebrated her 110th birthday this April.
     C) She moved to the U.S. at a young age.
     D) She is the world's oldest individual.

【题目】 What do we learn about Branyas Morera?

【翻译】 我们对Branyas Morera有什么了解?
           A) 她是世界上最著名的钢琴家之一。                                    B) 她在今年四月庆祝了她的110岁生日。

           C) 她在年轻时移居美国。                        D) 她是世界上最年长的个人。

【答案】 D （细节题） She is the world's oldest individual.

【定位】 Branyas Morera is now 115 years and 320 days old. That means she's been alive longer than
           any other living individual.
           布兰亚斯·莫雷拉现在 115 岁零 320 天。这意味着她比任何其他在世的人都活得更久。

【信号】 longer than 任何人即最年长。答案信号是比较结构 longer than any other living
           individual——“比任何其他在世者都活得久”就等于“世上最年长”，这种“er than any
           other”的极限表述是定位最高级类信息的标志。

【替换】 longer than any other living individual ↔ the world's oldest individual
           比较级换最高级

【排除】 A) 过度推断

           原文确实提到她弹钢琴，但只说弹琴是她长寿秘诀之一，从未说她是“世界知名钢琴家”；
           one of the world's best-known pianists把“会弹琴”拔高成“知名钢琴家”，是过度推断。
               借用原文：playing the piano from a young age
           B) 原词陷阱

           原文说她现在 115 years and 320 days old，是 115 岁，本选项却说她四月庆祝“110 岁生
           日”；它盗用了原文真实存在的出生日期“April 4th”的月份与年龄数字来拼凑，数字被偷换，
           听到“April”就选会中招。
               借用原文：115 years and 320 days old
           C) 与原文相反

           原文说她出生在美国，后来 her family moved to Spain——是搬去西班牙；本选项却说她
           “年幼时搬到美国”，把方向彻底说反了。它盗用“moved”“young child”等原词，只把目的地
           颠倒，专坑没听清方向的人。
               借用原文：her family moved to Spain




                        懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 43 -->

20. A) Being always active on social media.
   B) Doing regular and vigorous exercise.
   C) Playing the piano from a young age.
   D) Following a specific diet every day.

【题目】 What is one of Branyas Morera's secrets to living a long life?

【翻译】 Branyas Morera长寿的秘诀之一是什么?
         A) 在社交媒体上始终保持活跃。                               B) 进行规律且高强度的运动。                                   C) 从小弹钢琴。

         D) 每天遵循特定的饮食。

【答案】 C （细节题） Playing the piano from a young age.

【定位】 She also shares some of her secrets to living so long. These include playing the piano from a
         young age.
         她还分享了一些自己长寿的秘诀。这些秘诀包括从小弹钢琴。

【信号】 These include 引出秘诀清单。答案信号是列举句 These include playing the piano from
         a young age——“These include”紧跟在“her secrets”之后，明确把弹琴列入秘诀清单，
         这种“include/such as”的列举信号是细节题的金标定位点。

【替换】 playing the piano from a young age ↔ Playing the piano from a young age
         列举项原样对应

【排除】 A) 原词陷阱

         原文确实说她 active on social media，但这只是描述她的日常状态，紧接其后“These
         include”列举秘诀时并没有把“活跃于社交媒体”列进去；本选项盗用“active on social
         media”原词，却答非所问。
             借用原文：active on social media
         B) 偷换概念

         原文只说她 led an active lifestyle（生活方式活跃），并没说她做“regular and vigorous
         exercise（规律而剧烈的运动）”；本选项把“active lifestyle”偷换成程度更重的“vigorous
         exercise”，程度被拔高。
             借用原文：led an active lifestyle
         D) 与原文相反

         原文明确说她 has never followed a specific diet——从不遵循特定饮食；本选项却说她
         “每天遵循特定饮食”，与原文相反。它盗用“specific diet”原词，把否定听成肯定就会误选。
             借用原文：never followed a specific diet




                      懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 44 -->

21. A) Eating as little as one can possibly manage.
     B) Having the ability to forget things selectively.
     C) Figuring out the positive properties of each food item.
     D) Knowing how to remember major events in one's life.

【题目】 What does Branyas Morera think also contributes to a long life?

【翻译】 Branyas Morera认为还有什么有助于长寿?
           A) 尽可能少吃。               B) 有选择性地忘记事情的能力。                                     C) 弄清每种食物的有益特性。

           D) 知道如何记住人生中的重大事件。

【答案】 B （态度观点题） Having the ability to forget things selectively.

【定位】 She has also said that the secret to a long life is "knowing how to choose" what to forget.

           她还说过，长寿的秘诀是“懂得选择”要忘掉什么。

【信号】 she has also said 引出本人观点。答案信号是引述句 She has also said that the secret
           to a long life is——“she has also said”直接标记这是她本人的另一条观点，加上“also”正
           对应题干的“also contributes”，是态度观点题最可靠的定位信号。

【替换】 knowing how to choose what to forget ↔ the ability to forget things selectively
           选择遗忘换选择性遗忘

【排除】 A) 过度推断
           原文说她 always eaten little, but everything——吃得少但什么都吃；本选项却说“尽可能
           少吃”，把“eaten little”拔高到极端，还丢了“但什么都吃”的另一半。它盗用“eaten little”原
           词，制造“听到 little 就选”的诱惑。
               借用原文：always eaten little, but everything
           C) 偷换概念

           原文说酸奶对身体有 an infinite number of positive properties，是夸酸奶本身；本选项
           却说“弄清每种食物的有益属性”，把“酸奶有有益属性”偷换成“她认为要研究每种食物的属
           性”。它盗用“positive properties”原词，所指对象被偷换。
               借用原文：an infinite number of positive properties
           D) 与原文相反

           原文强调的是“懂得选择忘掉什么”，还说 Life would be impossible if we remembered
           everything——记住一切反而不行；本选项却说“懂得记住人生大事”，方向与原文相反。它
           盗用 remember/forget 同一话题词，把遗忘说成记忆。
               借用原文：Life would be impossible if we remembered everything
Questions 22 to 25 are based on the passage you have just heard.

1   It's the first day of school, and your teacher tells you to find a seat in the classroom. Where do you
sit? Does it matter? Yes. It does matter where you sit in your classroom, so choose and choose carefully.




                         懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 45 -->

Studies report that where you sit in the classroom can impact your ability to learn and the way your
teacher perceives you. Guess what else? Your grade.
这是开学第一天,老师让你在教室里找个座位。你会坐在哪里?这重要吗?是的。你坐在教室的哪个位置确实
很重要,所以要慎重选择。研究表明,你在教室里的座位会影响你的学习能力以及老师对你的看法。你猜还
有什么?你的成绩。

2   Then what is the best seat in the classroom?
那么教室里最好的座位是哪里呢?

3   In one study, students who sat in the front and center of the classroom received a higher grade
average and scored higher on tests than those sitting towards the back of the classroom. Get this.
Students sitting in the middle of the classroom also outscored students sitting behind them. This study
gathered that students had a clearer vision of the blackboard and were able to focus more attention on
the instructor. By sitting front and center, students were able to hear better too.

在一项研究中,坐在教室前排和中间位置的学生比坐在教室后排的学生获得了更高的平均成绩,考试分数也
更高。还有一点。坐在教室中间的学生的成绩也超过了坐在他们后面的学生。这项研究发现,学生们对黑板
有更清晰的视野,能够将更多注意力集中在授课教师身上。坐在前排和中间位置,学生们也能听得更清楚。

4   The instructors said that they also formed a better connection with the students who sat in front.
Perhaps the connection is made because of eye contact and visibility and more interaction. But when you
sit in the front row, you may be called on more by your teacher, mainly due to the fact that you are
easier to see. So for students who are not prepared and for those who are extremely shy, the attention
could cause some unwanted stress.
授课教师表示,他们与坐在前排的学生建立了更好的联系。也许这种联系是由于眼神交流、可见度以及更多
互动而形成的。但是当你坐在前排时,你可能会更多地被老师提问,主要是因为老师更容易看到你。所以对
于没有准备好的学生以及极度害羞的学生来说,这种关注可能会带来一些不必要的压力。

22. A) The ability to learn.
     B) The first day of school.
     C) How teachers perceive them.
     D) Where they sit in the classroom.

【题目】 What is of great importance to students according to the speaker?

【翻译】 根据讲话者的观点,什么对学生来说非常重要?
           A) 学习能力。            B) 开学第一天。                    C) 教师如何看待他们。                           D) 他们在教室里坐在哪里。

【答案】 D （细节题） Where they sit in the classroom.

【定位】 It does matter where you sit in your classroom, so choose and choose carefully. Studies report
           that where you sit in the classroom can impact your ability to learn and the way your teacher
           perceives you.
           你坐在教室的哪个位置，确实有影响，所以要慎重选座。研究表明，你在教室里的座位会影
           响你的学习能力以及老师对你的看法。




                        懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 46 -->

【信号】 加重强调 It does matter。说话人先自问 Where do you sit? Does it matter? 紧接着用加
          重语气的 It does matter where you sit in your classroom 自答，does 的强调正是答案信
          号——被反复强调的"坐在哪"就是说话人认定的重点。

【替换】 where you sit in your classroom ↔ Where they sit in the classroom.
          人称转换，意思不变

【排除】 A) 原词陷阱

          原文确实念到 your ability to learn，但那是"座位"带来的"影响之一"，并非说话人说的"对
          学生重要的东西"本身。把"被影响的结果"当成"重要的东西"，是典型的听到原词就想选。
              借用原文：can impact your ability to learn
          B) 原词陷阱

          原文开头说 It's the first day of school，但那只是用来引出话题的场景背景，说话人从没
          说"开学第一天"对学生重要，属于借开头原词设的陷阱。
              借用原文：It's the first day of school
          C) 答非所问

          原文 the way your teacher perceives you 确被念到，但它和"学习能力"一样，是座位带来
          的"影响"，不是说话人认定"重要"的那样东西，张冠李戴。
              借用原文：the way your teacher perceives you
23. A) They often received close attention from the instructors.
   B) They obtained the highest grade average in their class.
   C) They actually wanted a clear vision of the blackboard.
   D) They scored higher than students sitting behind them.

【题目】 What did one study find about students seated in the middle of the classroom?

【翻译】 一项研究发现坐在教室中间的学生有什么特点?
          A) 他们经常受到教师的密切关注。                                  B) 他们获得了班级中最高的平均成绩。

          C) 他们实际上想要清楚地看到黑板。                                    D) 他们的分数高于坐在他们后面的学生。

【答案】 D （细节题） They scored higher than students sitting behind them.

【定位】 Get this. Students sitting in the middle of the classroom also outscored students sitting
          behind them.
          注意了：坐在教室中间的学生，成绩也超过了坐在他们后面的学生。

【信号】 提示语 Get this 引出关键发现。说话人用 Get this（"注意听这个"）专门提醒，随后给出
          Students sitting in the middle of the classroom also outscored students sitting
          behind them，这种"提示语+发现"正是答案信号，直接对准"中间座位学生"的结论。

【替换】 outscored students sitting behind them ↔ They scored higher than students sitting behind them.
          outscore = scored higher than




                       懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 47 -->

【排除】 A) 张冠李戴

          原文 formed a better connection with the students who sat in front 讲的是"前排"学生
          与老师的关系，受关注的是前排不是"中间"。把前排学生的待遇安到"中间座位学生"头上，
          是张冠李戴。
             借用原文：formed a better connection with the students who sat in front
          B) 偷换概念

          原文只说前排/中间学生 received a higher grade average（比后排更高），选项却说成
          obtained the highest grade average in their class（全班最高），把"比较高"偷换成"最
          高"，程度被夸大。
             借用原文：received a higher grade average
          C) 偷换概念

          原文 students had a clearer vision of the blackboard 说的是前排中间学生"看黑板更清
          楚"这一既成事实（成绩高的原因），并非选项所谓"他们其实想要看清黑板（wanted）"，
          既改了意思又不是本题问的发现。
             借用原文：students had a clearer vision of the blackboard
24. A) A higher grade average among all students of a class.
   B) A better connection between instructors and students.
   C) A stronger support for a student-centered teaching approach.
   D) A more favorable atmosphere for effective classroom learning.

【题目】 What can eye contact, visibility and more interaction probably bring about?

【翻译】 眼神接触、可见度和更多互动可能会带来什么?
          A) 一个班级所有学生的平均成绩更高。                                      B) 教师与学生之间更好的联系。

          C) 对以学生为中心的教学方法更有力的支持。                                             D) 更有利于有效课堂学习的氛围。

【答案】 B （细节题） A better connection between instructors and students.

【定位】 The instructors said that they also formed a better connection with the students who
          sat in front. Perhaps the connection is made because of eye contact and visibility and
          more interaction.
          老师们说，他们和坐在前排的学生还建立了更好的联系。也许这种联系正是因为眼神交流、
          容易被看到以及更多的互动而形成的。

【信号】 因果倒装 because of 揭示因果。原文先给结果 formed a better connection，再用
          because of eye contact and visibility and more interaction 点出原因。题干把"因"列出
          来反问"带来什么果"，沿 because of 这条因果线，对应的"果"正是"更好的联系"。

【替换】 formed a better connection with the students who sat in front ↔
          A better connection between instructors and students.
          近乎原义概括，主体补全




                      懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 48 -->

【排除】 A) 答非所问

         a higher grade average 是上一段讲座位与成绩的内容，与本题问的"眼神交流等带来什
         么"不是同一个因果；选项还把范围扩成 among all students of a class（全班所有人），
         既答非所问又以偏概全。
             借用原文：received a higher grade average
         C) 未提及

         A stronger support for a student-centered teaching approach. 里"以学生为中心的教学
         法"原文从未提及，是凭"师生互动"硬联想出来的，属未提及。
         D) 过度推断

         A more favorable atmosphere for effective classroom learning. 听上去与"课堂学习"很
         搭，但原文讲的是"师生联系（connection）"，从没说"更有利的课堂学习氛围"，属未提及
         的合理推测项。


25. A) They may experience some unwanted stress.
   B) They may try hard to show their preparedness.
   C) They may receive extra attention from the instructor.
   D) They may feel helpless when called on by the instructor.

【题目】 What do we learn about extremely shy students sitting in the front row of
         the classroom?

【翻译】 关于坐在教室前排的极度害羞的学生,我们了解到什么?
         A) 他们可能会经历一些不必要的压力。                                       B) 他们可能会努力展示自己的准备情况。

         C) 他们可能会受到教师的额外关注。                                    D) 他们在被教师点名时可能会感到无助。

【答案】 A （细节题） They may experience some unwanted stress.

【定位】 So for students who are not prepared and for those who are extremely shy, the attention could
         cause some unwanted stress.
         所以，对于那些没准备好的学生以及那些极为害羞的学生来说，这种关注可能会带来一些不
         想要的压力。

【信号】 结尾 So 收束 + 转折隐含。前句还在说前排"被老师叫到更多"，结尾用 So for…those who
         are extremely shy, the attention could cause some unwanted stress 笔锋一转点出负面
         后果。So 引出的收束句正是针对"害羞学生"的答案落点。

【替换】 the attention could cause some unwanted stress ↔ They may experience some unwanted stress.
         cause→experience，因果换视角




                      懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 49 -->

【排除】 B) 与原文相反

       They may try hard to show their preparedness. 原文未提；原文恰恰说的是"没准备
       好"的学生 students who are not prepared，与"努力表现自己准备好了"方向相反。
           借用原文：students who are not prepared
       C) 原词陷阱

       the attention 确被念到，但原文说这关注对害羞学生造成的是"压力"；选项 They may
       receive extra attention from the instructor. 只截取"被额外关注"这个中性事实，回避了问
       的"后果"，是听到 attention 就想选的原词陷阱。
           借用原文：you may be called on more by your teacher
       D) 过度推断

       原文说前排学生 may be called on more（更可能被点名），但只说会带来"压力"，并未说
       他们"被点到时感到无助（feel helpless）"，是顺着"被点名"多推了一步。
           借用原文：you may be called on more by your teacher

SENTENCE INSIGHTS · 句子精讲


51   句真题句逐层精讲
下列真题句各有独立的句子解析页——中文翻译、结构树逐层拆解、成分与时态、考点讲
解与真题高频词。点击句子进入完整解析。




  That's more than double their $1.78 price in December, 2023.
  第2段，第2句

  主系表结构加比较级是常见句式，"more than" 引导的比较表达在真题中频繁出现，具有一定代表
  性，但本句结构过于简单，缺少从句或复杂修饰，典型性中等




  This was due to the risk of bird flu.
  第3段，第5句

  主系表结构 + 表原因的 "due to" 介词短语在考试中较常见，属于基础句式，但本句过于简短、无
  扩展成分，代表性一般。




                   懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 50 -->

Even so, there have been a very small number of cases recently when authorities
discovered eggs during further inspections.
第5段，第1句

there be 句型结合定语从句是四六级和考研阅读中的高频结构，尤其是用关系副词 "when" 引导定
语从句修饰表示时间或事件的名词（如 cases、situations）的句式非常典型。掌握这种结构对理解
新闻报道、学术文章等文体有较强的迁移价值。




What do we learn from the news report about the egg price in the U.S.?
第1题题干

结构典型性较高。特殊疑问句"疑问词 + 助动词 + 主语 + 动词原形"是四六级听力和阅读理解题中
的常见句式，掌握后可迁移至大量类似问句的理解和成分分析。




It has risen because of bird flu.
第1题，C

"主语 + 现在完成时谓语 + 原因状语" 是常见句式，在四六级和考研阅读中经常出现，具有一定代
表性，但结构过于简单，不足以覆盖复杂句式的典型特征。




Among the reasons given for the decision, the restaurant cited "noise levels, lack of
space for high chairs, cleaning up crazy messes, and the liability of kids running
around the restaurant".
第2段，第1句

涵盖主语、谓语、宾语、状语四种核心成分；宾语为并列的名词性短语且包含动名词，状语为含过
去分词后置定语的介词短语，成分种类较丰富；过去分词 "given" 作定语、动名词 "cleaning" 作宾
语、现在分词 "running" 作定语的区分，以及并列宾语的识别，都是值得练习的点




There are others, however, who showed support for the restaurant's decision.
第4段，第1句

there be 句型搭配限制性定语从句修饰主语的结构在四六级和考研阅读中频繁出现,是典型的长难句
基础模式,掌握后可迁移到大量类似句式的理解中,代表性较强。




                 懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 51 -->

What do we learn from the news report about the restaurant Nettie's House?
第3题题干

特殊疑问句在四六级听力和阅读理解题干中常见，"learn from" 搭配和介词短语后置定语也属高频
句式，具有一定复用价值。




It has banned kids under 10 from dining there.
第3题，B

本句采用 "主语 + 现在完成时谓语 + 宾语 + 介词短语状语" 的结构，在四六级和考研阅读中较为常
见，且 "ban ... from doing" 是高频搭配，具备一定代表性，但简单句结构本身迁移价值有限。




What do supporters say in response to the restaurant's new policy?
第4题题干

特殊疑问句在四六级和考研阅读、听力中属于常见句式，本句的 "疑问词 + do/does + 主语 + 动词
原形" 结构是标准提问模式，具有一定代表性，但作为独立简单句，句式偏短，缺少从句等更复杂
的典型考点。




They may carry viruses that are fatal to native snakes, which do not have
resistance.
第3段，第4句

本句展示了定语从句修饰名词的典型用法，尤其是限制性和非限制性定语从句的区别，这是四六
级和考研阅读中的高频句式。情态动词表可能性的用法也很常见，结构具有较强的代表性和迁移
价值。




If officers found the snake, they would employ a licensed snake catcher to catch it
and transport it to a specialist animal doctor, the government said.
第4段，第1句

间接引语 + 条件状语从句的组合是新闻报道和考试阅读中的高频句式，典型且实用性强




                懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 52 -->

If caught, the snake would be examined to determine where it came from, how
long it had been in the area, what it had been eating, whether it was carrying any
diseases of concern and whether it had produced babies.
第4段，第2句

这种“主句 + 目的状语 + 多个并列宾语从句”的结构在学术报告和长难句真题中极其常见，具有很
强的迁移价值。




What does the report say security officers were searching for?
第5题题干

特殊疑问句嵌套宾语从句、疑问词在从句中充当成分并提前到句首，这是四六级和考研阅读理解中
的高频句式。掌握这种结构后，可以迁移到大量类似的复杂疑问句，具有很强的典型性和实用性。




What did the government say would be done if security officers found what they
were searching for?
第7题题干

成分划分训练价值高。涵盖了多个易错点:疑问句中主谓宾的倒装识别,从句整体充当宾语的判定,条
件状语从句的定位,关系代词what的双重成分归属(引导词+从句内宾语),以及多层嵌套中各层主谓宾
的准确划分。特别适合训练学生在复杂句中逐层剥离、准确定位成分的能力




W: Well, it turns out that she's spent it all already.
第11段，第1句

"it turns out that" 结构在四六级和考研真题中频繁出现,属于高度典型的主语从句句式。掌握本句结
构后,学习者可以迁移到 "it happens that"、"it seems that" 等大量类似句型,实用性强。




You see, she was planning to quit the job she hates and retire early.
第15段，第2句

限制性定语从句省略关系代词、不定式并列作宾语、过去进行时表达过去计划，这些都是四六级和
考研真题中高频出现的句式特征，典型性较强，掌握后可广泛迁移




                 懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 53 -->

But now that she ran out of money, she can't.
第15段，第3句

"now that" 引导原因状语从句的结构在四六级和考研阅读中较为常见,且主句谓语省略的现象也频繁
出现,掌握后可复用到类似句式




W: She wants to, but her husband refuses to get rid of it.
第17段，第1句

"but" 连接的并列转折句是四六级和考研中的高频句式,两个对立观点的表达方式非常典型,掌握后可
大量复用于理解类似对话和短文。




He says the car is the best thing that has ever happened to him, and if she sells it,
he will divorce her.
第17段，第2句

全句结构在四六级和考研真题中高度典型：并列主句结构、宾语从句省略引导词、限制性定语从句
修饰先行词、条件状语从句与主句搭配，都是考试中的常见句式。第一个主句"says"后接宾语从句
且省略"that"，以及定语从句用"that"引导并充当从句主语，都是高频考点。掌握本句的结构分析方
法可以直接应用于大量类似真题句子，迁移性很强。




What do we learn about Hannah from the conversation?
第9题题干

特殊疑问句在四六级听力、阅读理解中频繁出现，尤其是询问 "从材料中了解到什么" 这类题型非
常典型，掌握后可大量复用于理解类似题目。




She no longer has any money left.
第9题，A

本句涵盖主语、谓语、宾语、宾语补足语、时间状语五种成分，成分种类相对丰富。宾语补足语
"left" 的识别是一个有价值的训练点，因为学习者容易将其误判为定语或与 "has" 构成完成时。时
间状语 "no longer" 插入主谓之间也是值得练习的位置变化。但句子整体结构较简单，缺少从句等
更复杂成分，训练价值中等偏上。




                 懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 54 -->

She can no longer hold on to her job.
第9题，C

本句体现 "情态动词 + 否定副词 + 动词短语 + 宾语" 的典型结构，在四六级和考研阅读中频繁出
现。短语动词 "hold on to" 和否定副词 "no longer" 的搭配是常考句式，掌握后可复用于类似表达
能力或状态变化的句子。




She has been robbed of 5 million Euros.
第9题，D

被动语态加介词短语状语的结构在四六级和考研阅读中较为常见，尤其是涉及 "be + 过去分词 +
of" 这类固定搭配的句型。学习者掌握后可以迁移到类似的被动句型中。但本句过于简短，缺少更
复杂的修饰或从句，代表性不如那些包含多重修饰或从句的典型长难句。




What does Hannah's husband say he'll do if she sells the new car?
第11题题干

疑问句 + 省略 that 的宾语从句 + 条件状语从句,是四六级和考研阅读中常见的组合句式。宾语从句
省略引导词、条件从句嵌套在宾语从句内,都是高频考点。掌握这个结构可以迁移到大量类似句子,
典型性很高。




I thought we agreed that we would not be sending him away at such a young age.
第2段，第2句

"think/believe/say等+宾语从句"以及"agree+宾语从句"都是四六级和考研阅读中的高频句式;两层
宾语从句嵌套的结构也很典型,学生掌握后可大量复用到其他认知动词和陈述动词的句子中,迁移
性强。




W: They do have a great outdoor campus where he could finally learn all those
outdoor sports you have been wanting him to try.
第3段，第1句

本句成分丰富,涵盖主谓宾基本结构、定语从句的关系副词 where 和省略关系代词两种引导方式、
宾语补足语 to try、程度状语 finally,以及完整的双宾语从句嵌套。省略关系代词的定语从句和 try
的宾语前置是两个重要的易错点,适合作为定语从句和成分划分的综合练习句,训练价值较高




                懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 55 -->

M: Yeah, the classrooms would probably smell like paint, making the air in the
school all stuffy.
第4段，第1句

涵盖主语、谓语、方式状语和结果状语四种成分，特别是现在分词引导的结果状语是常见易错点，
适合练习分词短语的识别和成分划分




They have a wonderful arts program that would really challenge Jake in terms of
creativity and imagination.
第4段，第3句

主句+限制性定语从句是四六级和考研阅读中的高频句式,that引导定语从句修饰宾语的结构极为常
见,掌握后可大量复用到其他句子的分析中。




Being practically next door, one of us could take him to school every day before
going to work.
第4段，第4句

本句体现了简单句中常见的句首状语（分词短语）+ 主谓宾 + 多个状语的典型结构，是四六级和
考研阅读中高频出现的句式。情态动词的使用以及多个状语的排列也符合真题中的常见模式，掌握
后可迁移到大量类似句子。




They have a reputation of being overly strict with the students, not to mention the
overwhelming amount of homework they assign, which would lead to a lot of
pressure for Jake and me.
第5段，第2句

本句覆盖主谓宾、定语从句（限制性和非限制性）、介词短语作定语和状语、关系代词省略等多种
成分和结构，涉及的语法点较为丰富，适合练习定语从句的识别和成分划分，训练价值较高




He is not yet mature enough to live on his own.
第12题，C

"主语 + be + 形容词 + enough + to do" 是考试中较常见的句型,用于表达某人或某物达到某种程度
以至于能够做某事,这种结构在四六级和考研阅读中经常出现,具有较好的典型性和复用价值，掌握
后可迁移到类似句型的理解。




                 懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 56 -->

Analytical decision-making is a decision-making approach where a manager
makes important business decisions only with solid data.
第1段，第1句

主系表结构加 where 引导的定语从句是四六级和考研阅读中的高频句式,常用于给专业术语下定义
或描述事物特征,学习者掌握后可迁移至大量同类定义句和说明句,具有较强的代表性




While this approach is beneficial when you have the time and the decision is
important, it is problematic when time is crucial.
第2段，第2句

本句涵盖主系表和主谓宾两种基本句型，包含让步状语从句、时间状语从句、并列结构等多种成
分，且存在嵌套关系。对于练习状语从句的识别、嵌套结构的分析、并列与从属的区分，有较高
价值。




This commitment, one right way of thinking or doing things, hinders the
manager's flexibility.
第3段，第3句

本句覆盖主语、谓语、宾语、同位语四种成分,同位语的识别(依靠逗号隔离和语义解释关系)是一个
有一定训练价值的考点,且同位语内部嵌套介词短语 "of thinking or doing things",可练习后置定语
的识别。但缺少定语从句、状语从句等更复杂的成分类型,整体训练收益中等。




Despite its restrictions, analytical decision-making does have several key
strengths.
第4段，第1句

"Despite + 名词短语" 作让步状语修饰主句是四六级和考研常见句式，具有一定代表性；助动词强
调结构也偶有出现，掌握后可迁移到类似的对比或转折语境中。




By relying on facts and data, you minimize your potential for a wrong decision.
第4段，第2句

句首介词短语 + 主谓宾核心结构是四六级和考研阅读中常见的句式，尤其是 by doing 引导方式状
语的用法出现频率高，掌握后有较好的迁移性。




                 懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 57 -->

What does the passage say is a major flaw of analytical decision-making?
第16题题干

特殊疑问句中嵌套宾语从句、疑问词在从句中充当成分,这是四六级和考研阅读理解题干的高频句
式。掌握这种结构后,可以直接迁移到大量类似的题目中,实用性极强。




Lay emphasis on thinking flexibly.
第17题，D

祈使句是四六级和考研写作中常用句式,尤其在建议类、说明文中频繁出现,具有较好的迁移性,但本
句结构过于简单,未涉及复杂修饰或从句,代表性中等。




Playing the piano is among the things the world's oldest person believes has
contributed to her long life.
第1段，第2句

本句涵盖了动名词短语作主语、系动词、介词短语作表语、省略关系代词的限制性定语从句、省略
连接词的宾语从句、现在完成时作谓语等多种成分和结构。特别是定语从句省略关系代词、宾语从
句省略that、以及从句嵌套这些点，都是学生容易混淆或忽略的地方，很适合用来训练对句子成分
的精细划分和层级关系的理解能力。




That means she's been alive longer than any other living individual.
第1段，第4句

"动词 + 省略 that 的宾语从句" 和 "现在完成时 + 比较状语" 都是四六级和考研常考结构。句式简洁
且实用性强，学习者掌握后可大量迁移到类似 "研究表明、数据显示、这意味着" 等常见表达，典
型性较高。




But her family moved to Spain, where they were originally from, when she was a
young child.
第2段，第2句

主句加非限制性定语从句加时间状语从句的组合是四六级和考研阅读中的常见句式，非限制性定语
从句补充背景信息、时间状语从句提供时间背景的用法非常典型，掌握后可广泛迁移到类似句子。




                 懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 58 -->

Unfortunately, she had to give up the practice seven years ago at 108.
第3段，第4句

简单句结构在真题中常见,"had to do" 情态表达和时间状语的配置也具有一定代表性,但因结构过于
简单,作为典型句式的学习迁移价值有限。




But she does eat yogurt every day, which she said has "an infinite number of
positive properties" for the body.
第4段，第5句

本句涵盖主谓宾、时间状语、非限制性定语从句、插入语等多种成分，且强调结构 does eat 和关
系代词 which 在从句中充当主语都是值得练习的考点。适合训练学生识别复杂修饰关系和插入成分
的能力。




"Life would be impossible if we remembered everything," she said.
第5段，第1句

结构高度典型。直接引语 + 引述动词是写作和考试中的常见句式,虚拟语气的 if 条件句(if + 过去式,
主句 would + 原形)是语法考点的经典结构。掌握后可大量迁移到类似的引述句和虚拟条件句中。




What does Branyas Morera think also contributes to a long life?
第21题题干

"特殊疑问句 + 宾语从句"的结构在四六级和考研阅读中非常常见,尤其是间接疑问句作宾语的用法
是高频考点;本句的疑问句语序和间接疑问句结构都是标准句式,掌握后可直接迁移到大量类似句子,
典型性很高




It's the first day of school, and your teacher tells you to find a seat in the
classroom.
第1段，第1句

并列句是四六级和考研阅读中的常见结构,"tell sb to do sth" 句型也很典型,但本句过于简单,缺少考
试中常见的修饰成分或复杂从句,代表性中等。




                 懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/listening/2025-12-2/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 59 -->

  It does matter where you sit in your classroom, so choose and choose carefully.
  第1段，第5句

  "It + 谓语 + 主语从句"的形式主语结构、主句并列用"so"表因果、祈使句省略主语，这三类结构在
  四六级和考研真题中均为高频句式；特别是形式主语结构和因果并列句是阅读和写作的常见考点，
  掌握后可大量复用。




  Students sitting in the middle of the classroom also outscored students sitting
  behind them.
  第3段，第3句

  涵盖了主语、谓语、宾语、状语、定语五种成分，其中两个后置定语的识别是典型练习点——需要
  区分现在分词短语作定语和作谓语的区别，这对基础较弱的学生是有价值的易错点；但缺少补语、
  从句等更复杂成分，训练广度有限。




  What can eye contact, visibility and more interaction probably bring about?
  第24题题干

  本句为典型的特殊疑问句结构，疑问词 "What" 作宾语提前，主语为并列名词短语，谓语为情态动
  词加动词原形。这类疑问句在四六级和考研阅读理解、翻译中频繁出现，掌握后可直接迁移至大量
  类似句式，代表性较强。




  What do we learn about extremely shy students sitting in the front row of the
  classroom?
  第25题题干

  结构典型性较高。特殊疑问句是考试中的高频句式，"What do we learn about..." 这种询问信息或
  观点的表达在阅读理解和听力材料中经常出现。现在分词短语作后置定语修饰名词也是常考语法
  点。掌握本句结构可以迁移到大量类似疑问句的分析中。

2025年12月·第2套 英语四级完形填空（选词填空） · 26–35 题答案
与解析

BLANKS & ANALYSIS · 选词填空题和解析


选词填空题和解析 · 26–35 题
本页 26–35 题的正确答案词与逐空解析由懒笔记按2025年12月·第2套 英语四级完形填空
（选词填空）原卷词库逐空核对整理。与试卷排版同一卷面：Directions、5 段带空短文
（每段之后紧随中文翻译）与 15 词共享词库连续排印；卷面下方逐空紧跟解析——该空
词性、原文定位句、正确词与锁定依据，以及同词性被淘汰竞争词的逐个归因。



真题卷第2套(2025-12) • 英语四级
Part Ⅲ Section A


Directions: In this section, there is a passage with ten blanks. You are required to select one word for
each blank from a list of choices given in a word bank following the passage. Read the passage through
carefully before making your choices. Each choice in the bank is identified by a letter. Please mark the
corresponding letter for each item on Answer Sheet 2 with a single line through the centre. You may not
use any of the words in the bank more than once.

    Some music inspires you to move your feet, some inspires you to get out there and change the
world. In any case, it's 26   to say that music moves people in special ways.
有些音乐激励你舞动双脚,有些音乐激励你走出去改变世界。无论如何,可以说音乐以特殊的方式打动
人们。

    If you're especially into a piece of music, your brain does something called Autonomous Sensory
Meridian Response (ASMR), which        27    to you like a sting in your brain.
如果你特别喜欢一段音乐,你的大脑会做一种叫做自发性知觉经络反应(ASMR)的事情,对你来说感觉就像大
脑中的一阵刺痛。

     It turns out that ASMR is pretty special. According to a      28     published study in The Journal
of Prevention of Alzheimer's Disease, the part of your brain responsible for ASMR doesn't get lost to
Alzheimer's. Alzheimer's     29      to put people into layers of confusion, and the study confirms that
music can sometimes       30    lift people out of the Alzheimer's haze and bring them back to (at least
a likeness of) normality … if only for a short while. ASMR is powerful stuff!
事实证明ASMR是相当特别的。根据最近发表在《阿尔茨海默病预防杂志》上的一项研究,你大脑中负责
ASMR的部分不会因阿尔茨海默病而丧失。阿尔茨海默病往往使人陷入层层困惑之中,而该研究证实音乐有
时实际上可以将人们从阿尔茨海默病的迷雾中解救出来,使他们恢复到(至少类似于)正常状态……即使只是
短暂的一会儿。ASMR是很强大的东西!

    This phenomenon has been       31     several times but rarely studied properly. One of the most
famous examples of this is the story of 92-year-old Henry Dryer, who comes out of dementia(        )                 痴呆

                    懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-a/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 75 -->

while listening to songs from his youth.
这种现象已被多次观察到,但很少得到适当的研究。其中最著名的例子之一是92岁的Henry Dryer的故事,
他在聆听自己年轻时的歌曲时从痴呆中清醒过来。

     Jeff Anderson, associate professor in Radiology at the University of Utah and      32    author on
the study, says "In our society, the diagnoses of dementia are    33     resources to the maximum. No
one says playing music will be a cure for Alzheimer's disease, but it might make the symptoms more
    34     , decrease the cost of care and improve a patient's  35     of life."
犹他大学放射学副教授、该研究的合著者Jeff Anderson说:"在我们的社会中,痴呆症的诊断正在最大限度
地消耗资源。没有人说播放音乐将是治愈阿尔茨海默病的方法,但它可能使症状更易于控制,降低护理成本
并改善患者的生活质量。"


                         A)     actually                            I)    phase
                         B)     consequently                        J)    plotting
                         C)     contributing                        K)     quality
                         D)     fair                                L)     recently
                         E)     feels                               M)      taxing
                         F)     manageable                          N)     tends
                         G)     mends                               O)     yielded
                         H)     observed


    26. it's __(26)__ to say that music moves people in special ways.

【答案】 D) fair          形容词

【词性槽】 it's ___ to say that …，形式主语 it 后系动词 + 表语

【依据】 It's fair to say 是英语高频习语「可以这样说、说……不为过」
          it's __(26)__ to say that music moves people in special ways. 前文用「有些音乐让你想
          跳舞，有些激励你改变世界」铺陈，再来一句「可以说音乐以特别的方式打动人心」做总
          结，正是 it's fair to say 的标准用法。

【竞争词】 F) manageable manageable 修饰「可控、可处理」，和 to say 接不上
            manageable 通常修饰任务、症状（make symptoms more manageable），用来说一件事
            「可控」；说一句话「可控」语义荒谬。

            M) taxing taxing 是「费神的」，说一句话费神在语境里不通

            taxing 多修饰任务、工作量；it's taxing to say 字面虽通，但与前文铺陈「音乐打动人」的语气
            完全相反，开篇就泼冷水不合逻辑。




                    懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-a/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 76 -->

    27. your brain does something called Autonomous Sensory Meridian Response (ASMR), which
__(27)__ to you like a sting in your brain.

【答案】 E) feels        动词

【词性槽】 your brain does … which ___ to you like a sting，which 引导的定语从句缺谓语

【依据】 feel like 是固定搭配「感觉像」
         which __(27)__ to you like a sting in your brain 后面跟 like a sting（像针刺），feels
         (to you) like … 正是「（对你来说）感觉像……」的标准表达，对人称 sb. 用 feels to sb.
         like 也通。

【竞争词】 G) mends mend 是「修补」，与 like a sting 不搭
           mend 通常修补衣服、关系，无法「mend to you like a sting」，搭配和语义都不成立。

           N) tends tend 后接 to do，不接 to you like

           tends 常见结构是 tends to do sth.（往往做某事），后面接动词原形；这里后面是介词 to you
           + like + 名词，结构对不上。

    28. According to a __(28)__ published study in The Journal of Prevention of Alzheimer's Disease

【答案】 L) recently            副词

【词性槽】 a ___ published study，冠词与过去分词之间填副词修饰 published

【依据】 recently published「最近发表」是学术文献标配搭配
         According to a __(28)__ published study，引用研究时强调「最近发表的一项研究」是英
         语中极高频的表达 a recently published study；时间副词 recently 与 published 在意义
         上紧密呼应。

【竞争词】 A) actually actually 表「实际上」，修饰 published 语义空洞
           actually published「实际上发表的」在英语里几乎不用——研究要么发表了要么没有，没有
           「实际上发表」这种说法，搭配不自然。

           B) consequently consequently 表因果，前文无因，逻辑不接

           consequently 用来连接前因后果，这里 According to a … published study 是引出依据，并非
           承接因果；且 a consequently published study 不是英语习惯搭配。

【易错】 最具迷惑性的错误词是 A) —— L 还是 A？三个副词都「看着能用」。a __(28)__
         published study 中考生可能凭直觉填 actually 表「实际上发表」，但英语习惯是 recently
         published；防坑点：a + 时间副词 + V-ed 名词是学术语境的强搭配模板，时间副词优先
         于强调副词。




                   懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-a/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 77 -->

   29. Alzheimer's __(29)__ to put people into layers of confusion

【答案】 N) tends          动词

【词性槽】 Alzheimer's ___ to put people …，主语后缺谓语，第三人称单数

【依据】 tends to do 表「倾向于、往往」，契合阿尔茨海默症常见症状

        Alzheimer's __(29)__ to put people into layers of confusion，「阿尔茨海默症往往让人
        陷入层层困惑」，tends to 表常发性、规律性，符合医学论述的口吻。

【竞争词】 G) mends mend 不接 to do，且语义反向

          mend 是「修补、痊愈」，不接 to do；而且 Alzheimer's mends people 意思是「阿尔茨海默
          症治愈人」，与下文「陷入困惑」完全矛盾。

   30. music can sometimes __(30)__ lift people out of the Alzheimer's haze

【答案】 A) actually            副词

【词性槽】 music can sometimes ___ lift people out of …，情态动词 + 副词 + 实义动词

【依据】 actually 强调「真的、的确」，呼应前文研究证实

        the study confirms that music can sometimes __(30)__ lift people out of the
        Alzheimer's haze，研究「证实」音乐有时确实能把患者从混沌中拉出来，actually 强调这
        种效果是真实存在的，与 confirms 的语气一致。

【竞争词】 B) consequently consequently 是因果连接副词，本句无因果链
          consequently 通常放句首/分句首引出结果（…, consequently, …）；这里没有「因为某事 →
          所以 lift」的因果，硬塞进 can sometimes consequently lift 既不合习惯也不合逻辑。

   31. This phenomenon has been __(31)__ several times but rarely studied properly.

【答案】 H) observed               动词

【词性槽】 This phenomenon has been ___ several times，被动语态，has been + 过去分词

【依据】 observed「被观察到」与并列的 studied 同属研究动作

        This phenomenon has been __(31)__ several times but rarely studied properly. 中 but
        连接转折，前后都讲科研动作：「被观察到多次，却很少被认真研究」，observed 与
        studied 是天然的近义并列。

【竞争词】 O) yielded yield 不及物或表「产生/屈服」，与 phenomenon 主语不搭

          yield 表「产生（结果）」时用法是 A yielded B；表「屈服」时是 yield to。This
          phenomenon has been yielded 字面意思「这种现象被产生了」语义荒谬，主被动语义都对
          不上。




                   懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-a/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 78 -->

     32. Jeff Anderson, associate professor in Radiology at the University of Utah and __(32)__ author
on the study

【答案】 C) contributing                  形容词

【词性槽】 ___ author on the study，冠词位 + 名词 author 前作定语

【依据】 contributing author 是学术文献固定身份「参与撰写的作者」

         associate professor in Radiology at the University of Utah and __(32)__ author on the
         study，介绍 Jeff Anderson 的双重身份，「副教授」+「该研究的参与撰写作者」逻辑自
         然；contributing author 是学术论文署名中的标准说法。

【竞争词】 J) plotting plotting 是「密谋/绘图」，修饰 author 语义荒诞

            plotting author 字面是「正在密谋的作者」或「在画图的作者」，作为学术身份描述完全不
            通；学术场合无此搭配。

            M) taxing taxing 形容「繁重的」，不能用来形容人的身份

            taxing 通常修饰任务、工作（a taxing job），用来形容 author（人）逻辑不通，且语义偏负
            面，与正面介绍学者的语境冲突。

    33. the diagnoses of dementia are __(33)__ resources to the maximum.

【答案】 M) taxing             动词

【词性槽】 the diagnoses of dementia are ___ resources，be 动词后缺谓语成分，与后面
            resources 形成动宾

【依据】 tax resources「使资源吃紧」是固定搭配，与 to the maximum 呼应

         the diagnoses of dementia are __(33)__ resources to the maximum，「痴呆诊断正在
         最大限度地消耗资源」；tax 作动词意为「使……负担沉重」，tax resources 在医疗/财政语
         境是标准说法，to the maximum「到极限」直接强化这种「榨干资源」的意味。

【竞争词】 J) plotting plot resources 不是搭配，且语义对不上「占用资源」

            plot 表「策划/绘制」，plot resources 字面像「策划资源、绘制资源」，与「消耗社会资源」
            的语境完全错位。

【易错】 最具迷惑性的错误词是 J) —— M 还是 J？「熟词僻义」盲区。are __(33)__ resources to
         the maximum 中考生若不知 tax 还有动词义「使吃紧」，会怀疑 taxing，转去试
         plotting；防坑点：看到 resources、energy、patience 等被「耗」对象，taxing 比
         plotting 自然得多，平时多积累一词多义。




                    懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-a/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 79 -->

   34. it might make the symptoms more __(34)__, decrease the cost of care

【答案】 F) manageable                  形容词

【词性槽】 make the symptoms more ___，make + 宾语 + 形容词比较级，空填形容词

【依据】 make symptoms more manageable「让症状更可控」医学语境标配

        it might make the symptoms more __(34)__, decrease the cost of care and improve a
        patient's __(35)__ of life.，三件好事并列：症状更可控、护理成本下降、生活质量提升；
        manageable 与「降低成本、改善质量」语气完全一致。

   35. improve a patient's __(35)__ of life

【答案】 K) quality            名词

【词性槽】 improve a patient's ___ of life，所有格+of 锁定名词

【依据】 quality of life 是英语固定搭配
        improve a patient's __(35)__ of life 中，quality of life「生活质量」是医学/社科语境里的
        高频固定表达，与前文 decrease the cost of care 并列，逻辑顺。

【竞争词】 I) phase phase of life 偏「人生阶段」，与「改善」不搭

          phase of life 表示「人生的某个阶段」，可以「进入」一个 phase 但谈不上 improve a phase
          of life；这里要的是生活的「品质」可以被提升，phase 在搭配上和语义上都对不上。

全文主旨


音乐能短暂唤回阿尔茨海默病人的清醒
全文围绕一项研究展开：负责 ASMR（自主感官经络反应）的大脑区域不会被阿尔茨海默病侵
蚀，于是音乐能短暂把患者从混沌中拉回来。开篇 Some music inspires you to move your
feet, some inspires you to get out there and change the world. 先用日常感受定调，再过渡到
the part of your brain responsible for ASMR doesn't get lost to Alzheimer's 这一核心发现；结
尾 it might make the symptoms more __(34)__, decrease the cost of care and improve a
patient's __(35)__ of life. 落到治疗与生活质量的现实意义。

<!-- source-page: 81 -->

DISTRACTORS · 干扰词复盘


这 5 个词始终没被任何空选用
词库 15 词里有 5 个是命题人埋的干扰词——它们词性看着也对、词义也沾点边，却不该
填进任何一个空。下面逐个剖析它们最像哪个空的答案、为什么是陷阱。




    B) consequently 最像 30 空 answer 的副词陷阱
    music can sometimes __(30)__ lift people out of the Alzheimer's haze 里如果误把研究证实「→ 因
    而音乐能起作用」当因果，就会想到 consequently；识破点：句中没有明确的因果链，confirms 后
    接结果是「证实结果」而非「因此导致」，应选强调副词 actually。



    G) mends 最像 29 空的动词陷阱
    Alzheimer's __(29)__ to put people into layers of confusion 中若不留意 mend 是否接 to do、且不
    查语义反向，可能被「mends」迷惑；识破点：mend 不接 to do，语义还是反方向（修复 vs 让人
    困惑）。



    I) phase 最像 35 空的名词陷阱
    improve a patient's __(35)__ of life 中 phase of life 是「人生阶段」，看起来也能拼成 's ___ of life
    的壳子；识破点：improve 搭配的是 quality 而非 phase——人生阶段无法「被改善」。



    J) plotting 最像 33 空的 V-ing 陷阱
    the diagnoses of dementia are __(33)__ resources to the maximum 里 plotting 也是 -ing 形且能跟
    宾语，乍看可填；识破点：plot resources 不是英语搭配，且与 to the maximum「消耗到极限」逻
    辑对不上。



    O) yielded 最像 31 空的过去分词陷阱
    This phenomenon has been __(31)__ several times 里 yielded 是过去分词形式，能套进 has been
    后；识破点：yield 表「产生」时主动用法是 A yielded B，被动用「现象被产生」逻辑荒诞，且与
    but rarely studied 的并列同义关系对不上。

3   句真题句逐层精讲

下列真题句各有独立的句子解析页——中文翻译、结构树逐层拆解、成分与时态、考点讲
解与真题高频词。点击句子进入完整解析。




  In any case, it's fair to say that music moves people in special ways.
  第1段，第2句

  形式主语加宾语从句的结构在四六级和考研阅读中高频出现,it is + 形容词 + to do + that从句的
  句式具有较强的典型性和可迁移性。掌握这种结构后,可以应用于大量类似句式的理解和写作,实用
  性强。




  It turns out that ASMR is pretty special.
  第3段，第1句

  It turns out that 结构在四六级和考研阅读中频繁出现，是典型的形式主语 + 主语从句句式。掌握
  这一结构后可迁移到 It seems that, It appears that 等类似句型，实用性强，代表性较高。




  One of the most famous examples of this is the story of 92-year-old Henry Dryer,
  who comes out of dementia(               痴呆) while listening to songs from his youth.
  第4段，第2句

  主句的 one of 结构加系表句型,以及非限制性定语从句修饰人物,都是四六级和考研阅读中的常见句
  式。while 引导的时间状语从句省略形式也是高频考点。整体结构具有较强的代表性和复用价值。

题答案与解析

STATEMENTS & ANALYSIS · 信息匹配题和解析


信息匹配题和解析 · 36–45 题
本页 36–45 题的段落匹配答案与逐句解析由懒笔记基于2025年12月·第2套 英语四级长篇
阅读（信息匹配）原卷逐题核对整理。与试卷排版同一卷面：先是 Directions 与 12 段字
母标号长文（逐段附中文翻译）；之后 10 句陈述逐句排列，每句英文陈述后紧接本句解
析——陈述句中译、信息所在段落、锚点强弱、定位句中英对照、陈述句与原文的同义改
写对照与易混相邻段辨析。



真题卷第2套(2025-12) • 英语四级
Part Ⅲ Section B


Directions: In this section, you are going to read a passage with ten statements attached to it. Each
statement contains information given in one of the paragraphs. Identify the paragraph from which the
information is derived. You may choose a paragraph more than once. Each paragraph is marked with a
letter. Answer the questions by marking the corresponding letter on Answer Sheet 2.

                          Can playing video games help you get a better job?

A) The gaming industry is larger than films and music combined, yet few of us are likely to list our
   achievements in playing computer games as work experience in our résumé. But why not?
   Businesses are waking up to the skills gamers can bring to the workplace.
   游戏产业的规模超过电影和音乐产业的总和,然而我们中很少有人会在简历中把玩电脑游戏的成就列为
   工作经验。但为什么不呢?企业正在意识到玩家能够为职场带来的技能。

B) One start-up is convinced that the skills learned by playing games — hard-won through years of
   training and battle — can be applied to real-life work situations. And Game Academy reckons its
   belief is backed up by hard data. Do you enjoy unusual puzzle games like Portal, or tower defence
   games like Defense Grid? The team has found that IT workers play those more than average. But if
   you prefer Civilization, Total War, or X-Com, where strategy and resource management are key, then
   you might have more in common with managers.
   一家初创公司确信,通过玩游戏学到的技能——经过多年训练和战斗艰难获得的技能——可以应用于现
   实生活的工作情境。Game Academy认为其信念得到了确凿数据的支持。你喜欢Portal这样不寻常的
   解谜游戏,或者Defense Grid这样的塔防游戏吗?该团队发现IT工作者玩这些游戏的频率高于平均水
   平。但如果你更喜欢Civilization、Total War或X-Com这些以策略和资源管理为核心的游戏,那么你可
   能与管理者有更多共同之处。

C) Game Academy's idea is simple: analyse gamers' habits from their online gaming profile, and offer
   courses in valuable skills that reflect their aptitudes — skills they can practise and refine in-game.




                    懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 87 -->

   And there is already a growing acceptance that gaming skills are transferable.
   Game Academy的理念很简单:从玩家的在线游戏档案中分析其游戏习惯,并提供反映其天赋的有价
   值技能课程——这些技能他们可以在游戏中练习和提升。而且人们已经越来越认可游戏技能是可迁
   移的。

D) Even the military is hiring gamers. "The ability to absorb information, react swiftly and coordinate
   actions whilst remaining calm under pressure are often attributes of people that are good at gaming,"
   according to a Royal Air Force spokesperson. Those skills are part of what the Royal Air Force is
   looking for "in a variety of roles". "Skills acquired through gaming can be very relevant to certain
   areas."
   甚至军方也在招聘玩家。据英国皇家空军发言人称,"吸收信息、快速反应并在压力下保持冷静的同时
   协调行动的能力,往往是擅长游戏的人具备的特质"。这些技能是英国皇家空军在"各种岗位"中寻找的一
   部分。"通过游戏获得的技能在某些领域可能非常相关。"

E) "There are plenty of soft skills that gamers can utilise in a professional setting, such as teamwork,
   problem solving and strategic planning," says Ryan Gardner, a regional director with Hays
   recruitment. But does that really mean your top 100 ranking in Overwatch should go down in your
   résumé? "It's about how you either make it relevant to the job you are applying for, or how it makes
   you more interesting as a potential employee," Mr Gardner says.
   Hays招聘公司的区域总监Ryan Gardner说:"玩家可以在职业环境中运用大量软技能,如团队合作、解
   决问题和战略规划。"但这真的意味着你在Overwatch中排名前100应该写进简历吗?"关键在于你如何
   让它与你申请的工作相关,或者它如何让你作为潜在员工更有趣,"Gardner先生说。

F) Two years ago, a Glasgow University study made headlines for suggesting gaming could make
   students more successful. But "the research hasn't really changed minds, at least not yet," says one of
   the authors of the study, Dr Matthew Barr. He's now writing a book on the topic. "For now, I think
   the negative reputation around games means that telling someone you're a determined gamer is more
   likely to harm your chances of getting a job. But if someone can explain how they've led a team in
   an online game, for example, they may be able to persuade an employer that this is useful," says Dr
   Barr.
   两年前,格拉斯哥大学的一项研究因提出游戏可以让学生更成功而成为头条新闻。但"这项研究并没有真
   正改变人们的想法,至少目前还没有,"该研究的作者之一Matthew Barr博士说。他现在正在写一本关于
   这个主题的书。"目前,我认为游戏的负面声誉意味着告诉别人你是一个坚定的玩家更可能损害你获得工
   作的机会。但如果有人能解释他们如何在网络游戏中领导一个团队,例如,他们可能能够说服雇主这是有
   用的,"Barr博士说。

G) Playing games casually is unlikely to help your career prospects. But part of Game Academy's
   suggestion is to transform casual gamers into "conscious" ones, applying critical thinking to
   developing their skills. "We see gameplay as a resource of talent," says co-founder David Barrie.
   "Why can't we put in our résumé our gaming victories and achievements?" he asks. "If they say they
   want leadership — why can't I point to my years of leading World of Warcraft raids?"
   随意玩游戏不太可能帮助你的职业前景。但Game Academy建议的一部分是将休闲玩家转变为"有意
   识的"玩家,运用批判性思维来发展他们的技能。联合创始人David Barrie说:"我们将游戏玩法视为人才
   资源。""为什么我们不能在简历中写上我们的游戏胜利和成就?"他问道。"如果他们说他们想要领导力
   ——为什么我不能指出我多年领导World of Warcraft团队副本的经历?"



                    懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

H) There are already plenty of gamers out there who know that their hobby has made them who they
   are. Earlier this year, Matthew Ricci told gaming site Kotaku: "If you're playing EVE Online you
   basically already have an MBA." Often made fun of in gaming circles as a "spreadsheet simulator(                   电
   子表格模拟        )", the economy of the fictional EVE universe is driven by real market principles. If you
   want to build a new spaceship, the raw material has to be mined by another player. Manufacturing
   costs come into effect, and commodities fluctuate in price based on demand and the distance to haul
   the commodity.
   已经有很多玩家知道他们的爱好塑造了他们。今年早些时候,Matthew Ricci告诉游戏网站Kotaku:"如
   果你在玩EVE Online,你基本上已经拥有了MBA学位。"EVE经常在游戏圈中被嘲笑为"电子表格模拟
   器",这个虚构EVE宇宙的经济由真实的市场原则驱动。如果你想建造一艘新飞船,原材料必须由另一个
   玩家开采。制造成本发挥作用,商品价格根据需求和运输商品的距离而波动。

I) Mr Ricci, who had always dreamed of being the boss of his own company, ran an in-game
   corporation comprising hundreds of players. Eventually, he realised he could transfer his skills to real-
   life business — instead of doing it for free. He restructured Zentech, once a taxation vehicle for his
   father's business, and it is now in its fourth year helping international brands enter the Canadian
   market. EVE teaches skills like creativity, leadership, organisation, and conflict resolution, he
   maintains. He credits his success to his family, his strong desire to run his own business — and "a
   damn good company in Iceland that made a damn good game".
   一直梦想成为自己公司老板的Ricci先生经营着一个由数百名玩家组成的游戏内公司。最终,他意识到自
   己可以将技能转移到现实生活的商业中——而不是免费做这件事。他重组了Zentech,这家曾经是他父
   亲生意的避税工具的公司,现在已进入第四年帮助国际品牌进入加拿大市场。他坚持认为,EVE教授了创
   造力、领导力、组织能力和冲突解决等技能。他将自己的成功归功于家人、强烈的经营自己企业的愿望
   ——以及"冰岛一家该死的好公司制作的一款该死的好游戏"。

J) Digital entrepreneur and business consultant Mia Bennett says: "In more traditional settings, gaming is
   still imagined to be the pursuit of teenage boys — a waste of time." But there are some links to
   skills like "decision-making, the ability to anticipate and future planning," she says. "It also helps with
   meta-skills — learning how to learn, experimentation, and creative thinking."
   数字企业家和商业顾问Mia Bennett说:"在更传统的环境中,游戏仍然被认为是十几岁男孩的追求——浪
   费时间。"但它与"决策、预测能力和未来规划"等技能存在一些联系,她说。"它还有助于元技能——学
   习如何学习、实验和创造性思维。"

K) Twelve years into his career, Mark Long, a radiotherapy physicist with the NHS in Surrey, doesn't
   get as much gaming time as he used to. "I would love to say that my gaming skills allow me to
   destroy cancer using linear accelerators like photon(                  光子
                                                               ) blasters, but that's not quite the reality."
   Instead, he credits old-school games like Palace of Magic with exposing him to computers. Every
   new gaming upgrade improved his knowledge of how they worked. Gaming also encouraged a
   competitive spirit — and that, he believes, translates. "When creating treatment plans, the aim is to
   optimise the radiation dose to the tumour and restrict it as much as possible to healthy surrounding
   tissues and organs," he says. It's about "repeating the process, but each time doing something slightly
   different to improve the result". Not unlike achieving a high score or a perfect run. "Most
   videogames are essentially puzzles to solve," he says. "And problem-solving is a big part of my job."
   在职业生涯的第十二年,Surrey郡NHS的放射治疗物理学家Mark Long没有以前那么多游戏时间
   了。"我很想说我的游戏技能让我能够像使用光子爆破器一样用直线加速器摧毁癌症,但现实并非如




                     懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 89 -->

   此。"相反,他认为Palace of Magic这样的老式游戏让他接触到了计算机。每次游戏升级都提高了他对
   计算机工作原理的了解。游戏还培养了竞争精神——他认为这是可以转化的。"在制定治疗计划时,目
   标是优化肿瘤的辐射剂量,并尽可能限制对周围健康组织和器官的辐射,"他说。这是关于"重复这个过程,
   但每次做一些略有不同的事情来改善结果"。这与获得高分或完美通关没什么不同。"大多数电子游戏
   本质上都是需要解决的谜题,"他说。"而解决问题是我工作的重要部分。"

L) "Over at Game Academy, Mr Barrie is aware they still have a mountain to climb. The employer
   community will need the science," he says, but confesses he only became a true believer when he
   started asking players about their biggest in-game achievements. "The scale and scope of their
   answers were amazing," he says. "But ask them the same about their work, and they don't know."
   "The engagement and powerful skills that people get playing a game — why can't work be like
   that?" he asks.
   在Game Academy,Barrie先生意识到他们仍有一座山要攀登。他说,雇主群体将需要科学依据,但他承
   认只有当他开始询问玩家他们在游戏中最大的成就时,他才成为真正的信徒。"他们回答的规模和范围令
   人惊叹,"他说。"但问他们关于工作的同样问题,他们却不知道。""人们玩游戏时获得的参与感和强大技
   能——为什么工作不能像那样?"他问道。


陈述句与逐句解析

36. Certain gaming skills meet the requirements of the armed forces in Britain.

某些游戏技能符合英国武装部队的要求。
【答案】 D 段（强锚点）

【锚点】 英国武装部队

【定位】 Even the military is hiring gamers. "The ability to absorb information, react swiftly and
          coordinate actions whilst remaining calm under pressure are often attributes of people that are
          good at gaming," according to a Royal Air Force spokesperson.
          连军方都在招玩家。皇家空军发言人说：吸收信息、快速反应、在压力下保持冷静同时协调
          行动的能力，往往是擅长打游戏的人身上具备的特质。

【改写】 the armed forces in Britain ↔ Royal Air Force
          上位 vs 下位的具体代表

          Certain gaming skills meet the requirements ↔

          Skills acquired through gaming can be very relevant to certain areas
          meet the requirements 与 be relevant to certain areas 同义


37. One veteran player has applied his gaming skills to his real-world business and owes his success
    partly to a game company.

一位资深玩家将其游戏技能应用于现实世界的商业活动,并将成功部分归功于一家游戏公司。
【答案】 I 段（强锚点）

【锚点】 老玩家 + 把成功部分归功于游戏公司

<!-- source-page: 90 -->

【定位】 He credits his success to his family, his strong desire to run his own business — and "a damn
          good company in Iceland that made a damn good game".
          他把自己的成功归功于家人、强烈的自主创业愿望，以及一家在冰岛做出好游戏的好公司。

【改写】 has applied his gaming skills to his real-world business ↔

          he realised he could transfer his skills to real-life business
          apply ... to 与 transfer ... to 同义

          owes his success partly to a game company ↔

          He credits his success to his family, his strong desire to run his own business — and "a damn
          good company in Iceland that made a damn good game"
          credit success to 与 owe success to 同义；partly 体现在并列归因

【辨邻】 G         Why can't we put in our résumé our gaming victories and achievements? 只是建
               议玩家用游戏成就求职，并没有出现 Ricci 那种把虚拟公司经验落到 Zentech 的实际
               创业故事，更没有把功劳归给游戏公司，与题干两个并列条件都对不上。
                 Why can't we put in our résumé our gaming victories and achievements?

38. According to a researcher, bad reputation about games will probably limit a gamer's chances of
    landing a job.

据一位研究人员称,游戏的负面声誉可能会限制游戏玩家获得工作的机会。
【答案】 F 段（强锚点）

【锚点】 研究者 + 坏名声会限制求职

【定位】 For now, I think the negative reputation around games means that telling someone you're a
          determined gamer is more likely to harm your chances of getting a job.
          目前我觉得游戏的负面名声意味着，跟别人说自己是个铁杆玩家更可能损害你找工作的
          机会。

【改写】 bad reputation about games ↔ the negative reputation around games
          bad 与 negative 同义；about 与 around 此处用法相同

          limit a gamer's chances of landing a job ↔ harm your chances of getting a job
          limit 与 harm 同义；landing a job 与 getting a job 同义

PARAGRAPH MAP · 段落主旨图


12   个段落各讲了什么
逐段一句主旨 + 该段最具区分度的「信息指纹」，是逐句回扫定位的工作台。




    A   用一个对比抛出全文话题：游戏产业体量已经超过电影加音乐，但几乎没人把游戏成
        就写进简历；不过企业开始意识到玩家身上有可用的职场技能。

         信息指纹       游戏产业体量对比 + 企业开始觉醒



    B   介绍创业公司 Game Academy 的核心信念：游戏里磨出来的技能可以迁移到真实工
        作；并用数据指出玩不同类型的游戏对应不同职业倾向，玩策略与资源管理类游戏的
        人更可能具有管理者气质。

         信息指纹       玩家类型与职业倾向数据对应




                   懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 95 -->

C   概述 Game Academy 的具体做法：分析玩家在线档案里的游戏习惯，据此提供能让玩
    家继续在游戏里练的技能课程；并指出社会已经越来越接受游戏技能的可迁移性。

    信息指纹    Game Academy 的运作流程



D   举英国军方的例子证明用人单位真的在招玩家：皇家空军发言人指出玩家身上的吸收
    信息、快速反应、压力下保持冷静等特质，正好符合多种岗位的要求。

    信息指纹    英国皇家空军招募玩家



E   Hays 招聘的区域总监 Ryan Gardner 指出玩家具备团队合作、解决问题、战略规划等
    软技能；至于要不要把游戏战绩写进简历，关键看它能不能贴合目标岗位、或让你这
    个候选人更有吸引力。

    信息指纹    招聘专家谈简历该不该写游戏战绩



F   Glasgow 大学研究作者 Matthew Barr 博士承认两年前的研究并没真正改变大众看法：
    当下游戏的负面名声反而可能害到求职者；但如果玩家能讲清楚自己在游戏里如何带
    队，仍有机会说服雇主这是有用的。

    信息指纹    研究者承认游戏负面名声拖累求职



G   随便玩玩对求职没有帮助；Game Academy 的主张是把随便玩的玩家转化为有意识的
    玩家——用批判性思维去发展技能，把游戏里的胜利与成就当作简历内容，把多年带
    魔兽世界副本团当成领导力证据。

    信息指纹    把休闲玩家转化为有意识玩家



H   已经有不少玩家清楚自己的爱好塑造了今天的自己；以 EVE Online 为例，它被戏称为
    电子表格模拟器，游戏里的经济完全按真实市场原理运转——造飞船要别人挖原料、
    要算制造成本、商品价格还会随需求与运距波动。
    信息指纹    EVE Online 的市场化经济系统



I   Ricci 在 EVE 游戏里经营过一家上百号人的虚拟公司，后来把这套本事用到现实，重整
    了 Zentech 这家原本只是父亲生意的避税壳公司，如今帮国际品牌进入加拿大市场已
    经第四年；他把成功部分归功于冰岛那家做出好游戏的公司。

    信息指纹    Ricci 把游戏经验落到 Zentech 业务




           懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 96 -->

   J   数字创业者兼商业顾问 Mia Bennett 指出在更传统的看法里游戏仍被想成是青少年男
       生的爱好、是浪费时间；但她随后也承认游戏对决策、预见、未来规划，以及学习如
       何学习等元技能有帮助。

        信息指纹     传统观念把游戏视为浪费时间



   K   NHS 放疗物理师 Mark Long 反思自己十二年的职业：是老牌游戏 Palace of Magic 最
       早把他领进计算机的世界，每次硬件升级都加深他对电脑的认识；游戏还培养了竞争
       心和反复优化的思路，与他给肿瘤设计放疗方案、不断改进结果的工作一脉相承——
       大多数电子游戏本质就是要解的谜，而解决问题正是他的核心工作。
        信息指纹     放疗物理师把解谜思维带进治疗方案



   L   Game Academy 的 Barrie 承认要让雇主社群信服仍有一座山要爬，雇主需要科学证
       据；他自己也是去问了玩家他们最大的游戏内成就之后才真正信了——玩家谈起游戏
       成就时滔滔不绝，谈起工作里的成就反而一脸茫然，他由此反问：为什么工作不能像
       玩游戏那样令人投入？

        信息指纹     雇主社群仍需要科学证据



SENTENCE INSIGHTS · 句子精讲



22     句真题句逐层精讲
下列真题句各有独立的句子解析页——中文翻译、结构树逐层拆解、成分与时态、考点讲
解与真题高频词。点击句子进入完整解析。




  The gaming industry is larger than films and music combined, yet few of us are
  likely to list our achievements in playing computer games as work experience in
  our résumé.
  A段，第1句

  并列转折句在四六级和考研阅读中较为常见，"yet" 连接对比分句的结构具有一定代表性，适合作
  为理解转折逻辑和对比关系的练习句，但句式本身不属于高频难点句型。




                懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 97 -->

But if you prefer Civilization, Total War, or X-Com, where strategy and resource
management are key, then you might have more in common with managers.
B段，第5句

条件状语从句（if引导）+ 主句是常考句式，尤其在逻辑推理类阅读中频繁出现。非限制性定语从
句对先行词进行补充说明，也是真题常见结构。情态动词"might"表达推测，符合议论文、说明文
的典型表达方式。整体结构具有较高的可复用性，适合作为典型句式练习。




Game Academy's idea is simple: analyse gamers' habits from their online gaming
profile, and offer courses in valuable skills that reflect their aptitudes — skills they
can practise and refine in-game.
C段，第1句

句子使用冒号引出解释说明、定语从句修饰名词、破折号补充同位成分,这些都是四六级和考研阅
读中的高频句式。并列动词和嵌套修饰的组合也很常见,掌握后可在真题中大量复用。




"The ability to absorb information, react swiftly and coordinate actions whilst
remaining calm under pressure are often attributes of people that are good at
gaming," according to a Royal Air Force spokesperson.
D段，第2句

主句采用主系表结构，主语带复杂定语修饰，表语中嵌套限制性定语从句，这种结构在四六级和考
研阅读中高频出现；句子展示了如何用不定式并列、定语从句层层修饰来表达复杂概念，是典型的
学术或正式文体句式，掌握后可迁移到大量类似长难句的分析中。




Those skills are part of what the Royal Air Force is looking for "in a variety of
roles".
D段，第3句

主系表结构配合宾语从句是四六级和考研阅读中的常见句式。"part of + 宾语从句"以及 "what" 引
导的名词性从句都是高频结构。句子长度和复杂度也接近真题中的标准句型，学习后可迁移到大量
类似句子。




               懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 98 -->

"There are plenty of soft skills that gamers can utilise in a professional setting,
such as teamwork, problem solving and strategic planning," says Ryan Gardner, a
regional director with Hays recruitment.
E段，第1句

引语倒装结构、"There be" 存在句、限制性定语从句修饰主语，以及同位语补充说明，这些都是四
六级和考研真题中的高频句式。掌握本句的结构分析方法后，可迁移到大量类似句型，典型性强。




"It's about how you either make it relevant to the job you are applying for, or how
it makes you more interesting as a potential employee," Mr Gardner says.
E段，第3句

直接引语 + 并列宾语从句 + 定语从句省略关系代词 + 使役动词宾补结构,这些都是四六级和考研阅
读中的高频句式,尤其并列宾语从句和省略型定语从句的组合非常典型,掌握后可广泛应用于同类长
句的拆解。




But "the research hasn't really changed minds, at least not yet," says one of the
authors of the study, Dr Matthew Barr.
F段，第2句

直接引语 + 倒装句式（says + 主语）是学术文献和新闻报道中常见的引述结构，现在完成时否定
形式也是四六级和考研阅读高频时态；掌握后可迁移到大量类似句式。




"For now, I think the negative reputation around games means that telling
someone you're a determined gamer is more likely to harm your chances of getting
a job. But if someone can explain how they've led a team in an online game, for
example, they may be able to persuade an employer that this is useful," says Dr
Barr.
F段，第4句

综合其复杂的嵌套结构和典型的新闻报道文体，该句非常适合用于进阶的长难句结构分析训练。




               懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 99 -->

But part of Game Academy's suggestion is to transform casual gamers into
"conscious" ones, applying critical thinking to developing their skills.
G段，第2句

本句涵盖主语(含介词短语作定语)、系动词、表语(不定式短语，内含宾语和补语)、方式状语(现在
分词短语，内含动名词作介词宾语)，成分种类较丰富。不定式作表语与分词作状语是常见易混
点，具有一定训练价值，但整体难度不高，挑战性有限。




"We see gameplay as a resource of talent," says co-founder David Barrie.
G段，第3句

直接引语加says引述框架是新闻报道和采访报道中的常见句式，在四六级阅读理解和考研英语的新
闻类文本中频繁出现，具有较好的迁移性。




Often made fun of in gaming circles as a "spreadsheet simulator(                                      电子表格模拟)",
the economy of the fictional EVE universe is driven by real market principles.
H段，第3句

句子结构高度典型：前置过去分词短语作状语 + 被动语态主句 + 介词短语作方式状语，这种模式
在四六级和考研阅读中频繁出现，掌握后可大量复用于理解类似长句




Mr Ricci, who had always dreamed of being the boss of his own company, ran an
in-game corporation comprising hundreds of players.
I段，第1句

非限制性定语从句插入主句主语之后是英语书面语中的高频句式,在四六级和考研阅读中经常出
现。本句结构 "名词 + 非限制性定语从句 + 谓语 + 宾语" 是典型的复合句模式,掌握这一结构后可
以复用到大量类似句子的分析中,具有很强的迁移价值。




Twelve years into his career, Mark Long, a radiotherapy physicist with the NHS in
Surrey, doesn't get as much gaming time as he used to.
K段，第1句

本句覆盖了时间状语、主语、同位语、谓语、宾语（含比较结构）以及比较状语从句，成分种类丰
富。特别是同位语和比较从句中的省略现象（"used to" 后省略 "get"）是练习中的常见易错点，有
较好的训练价值。




               懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 100 -->

"I would love to say that my gaming skills allow me to destroy cancer using linear
accelerators like photon(       光子) blasters, but that's not quite the reality."
K段，第2句

本句覆盖了主谓宾、主系表、宾语从句、宾语补足语、方式状语等多种成分，且第一个主句的宾语
从句内部结构较为丰富（"allow somebody to do" 加现在分词修饰），适合练习识别嵌套结构中的
成分归属，尤其是区分宾语、宾语补足语和状语。




Instead, he credits old-school games like Palace of Magic with exposing him to
computers.
K段，第3句

本句涉及主谓宾基本框架,外加句首状语、后置定语和 credit A with B 这一固定搭配结构,成分种类
较丰富;宾语中包含介词短语作定语,with 后接动名词短语,适合练习识别固定搭配和区分不同介词短
语的作用,有一定训练价值




"When creating treatment plans, the aim is to optimise the radiation dose to the
tumour and restrict it as much as possible to healthy surrounding tissues and
organs," he says.
K段，第6句

本句覆盖了引述结构（直接引语作宾语）、系动词的主系表结构、时间状语短语（现在分词短
语）、并列不定式作表语等多种成分；值得练习的点包括：识别直接引语的边界、区分状语短语和
从句、处理并列不定式中的 "to" 省略现象；成分种类较丰富，有一定训练价值。




It's about "repeating the process, but each time doing something slightly different
to improve the result".
K段，第7句

涵盖主系表结构和介词短语作表语,适合练习系动词句型和表语的识别。但成分种类较单一,缺少宾
语、定语从句、复杂状语等易错成分,训练价值有限。




              懒笔记 · https://english-exam.lazynote.cn/cet4/sections/2025-12-2/part3-section-b/?f=p · 在线使用效果更好

<!-- source-page: 101 -->

  One veteran player has applied his gaming skills to his real-world business and
  owes his success partly to a game company.
  第37题题干

  本句包含主语、两个并列谓语、宾语、多个状语（介词短语和副词），成分种类较为丰富。并列谓
  语结构是常见易错点（学生易误判为并列分句），介词短语 "to his real-world business" 和 "to a
  game company" 的状语定位也有练习价值。但无从句或复杂嵌套，挑战性有限。




  Businesses are beginning to realise gaming skills are applicable to their employees'
  work.
  第39题题干

  "主句 + 省略 that 的宾语从句" 是四六级和考研阅读中高频出现的句式,且从句本身又是系表结构搭
  配介词短语,这种双层结构在真题中具有较强代表性,掌握后可迁移到大量类似句子。




  One who enjoys playing games that require management skills to win might
  possess more qualities of a manager.
  第42题题干

  本句成分丰富：主句包含主语（带定语从句）、谓语（情态动词结构）、宾语；第一个定语从句包
  含主语（关系代词）、谓语、宾语（动名词短语，内含定语从句修饰）；第二个定语从句包含主语
  （关系代词）、谓语、宾语、目的状语。这种多层次成分组合适合训练学生识别嵌套结构中的成分
  归属能力，并练习关系代词在从句中的双重作用。




  It is suggested that gaming skills be included in a résumé if they enhance a job
  applicant's appeal.
  第44题题干

  本句结构典型且高频,涵盖形式主语、主语从句、虚拟语气、被动语态、条件状语从句等多个核心
  考点,主干识别和嵌套层次都有一定难度,成分划分训练价值极高。虽然时态语态组合较复杂,但正因
  如此才更值得重点练习。对于备考四六级和考研的学习者来说,这是必须优先掌握的句型,综合训练
  价值突出。
